# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-27 through 2026-12-03  
**Updated:** 2026-11-27T11:04:26+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-27

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-30 — Bryant Park Winter Village Ice Skating + Kuroneko

**Bryant Park Winter Village Ice Skating, then a meal.** Japanese and Asian-influenced ramen at Kuroneko. It is 1.7 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 8 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Bryant+Park%2C+between+Fifth+and+Sixth+Avenues+and+40th+and+42nd+Streets%2C+New+York&destination=218+East+10th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/activities/the-rink) · [Event booking](https://book.peek.com/w/6e2f0590-d64d-4e77-b368-a874c585b1d2/WjgE7) · [Restaurant](https://www.kuroneko.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/kuroneko)

### 2026-11-27 — Columbus + Kuroneko

**Columbus, then dinner.** Japanese and Asian-influenced ramen at Kuroneko. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=7+Ludlow+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&destination=218+East+10th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://metrograph.com/film/9999005076-columbus/) · [Event booking](https://t.metrograph.com/Ticketing/visSelectTickets.aspx?cinemacode=9999&txtSessionId=31414) · [Restaurant](https://www.kuroneko.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/kuroneko)

### 2026-12-01 — Columbus Circle Holiday Market Opening Day + Café Fiorello

**Columbus Circle Holiday Market Opening Day, then a meal.** Italian at Café Fiorello. It is a 5-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Southwest+entrance+to+Central+Park%2C+Columbus+Circle%2C+New+York&destination=1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://urbanspacemarkets.com/pages/columbus-circle) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafefiorello.com/)

### 2026-11-28 — Beginners: brunch screening + Sofreh

**Beginners: brunch screening, then a meal.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12:15 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=75+Saint+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/beginners/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24875721/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/sofreh)

### 2026-11-28 — Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson + Café Paulette

**Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson, then dinner.** French bistro at Café Paulette. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 4 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=376+9th+Street&destination=1+South+Elliott+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.viewcy.com/event/found_sound_nation_pr_6) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafepaulette.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-paulette)

### 2026-12-01 — Pop-Up Concert: Steven Beck, piano + Café Fiorello

**Pop-Up Concert: Steven Beck, piano, then dinner.** Italian at Café Fiorello. It is 2.7 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=2960+Broadway&destination=1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.millertheatre.com/events/steven-beck-piano) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafefiorello.com/)

### 2026-11-29 — Brooklyn Flea in DUMBO + Altbau Bakery

**Brooklyn Flea in DUMBO, then a meal.** Bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=80+Pearl+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynflea.com/) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-12-03 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Café Paulette

**NYC Resistor Public Craft Night, then dinner.** French bistro at Café Paulette. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=1+South+Elliott+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nycresistor.com/) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafepaulette.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-paulette)

## Where to eat

NYC dining picks for November 27, 2026: weekday deals, seasonal menus, recent openings and upcoming bakery dates. Holiday service and availability are not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-27T10:44:25+00:00

No candidate's current Michelin distinction was independently verified against Michelin's own listings, so recognition fields remain empty. Restaurant biographies and historical stars do not establish current awards; guide selection is not a star.

### Le Gratin: weekday prix fixe and bar-only Et Frites

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

Choose a two- or three-course weekday lunch, or an early-evening bar meal with separately priced mini martinis.

Price: Lunch: 2 courses $45; 3 courses $55. Happy hour: selected entrees $30; mini martinis $8 each.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday-Friday 11:30am-2:30pm. Happy hour Tuesday-Friday 5-7pm.  
Conditions: Happy hour is at the bar; walk-ins welcome. The $30 entree does not include a martini. Lunch supplements include steak tartare +$5, skate +$3, cheeseburger +$5 and steak frites +$8. Tax, gratuity, minimum purchases and holiday eligibility are not specified.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/experiences)

### Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square: afternoon and late-night oysters

Deals & happy hours — Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square

An inexpensive seafood stop near theaters, with different oyster offers before dinner and later at the bar.

Price: Happy-hour oysters or littleneck clams $2 each, minimum 6; mini fish taco $4; mini chowder $4; selected cocktails and wines $12; beers $9. Separate late offer: chef-choice oysters $1.50 each.  
Schedule: Menu lists Monday 3:30pm-close and Tuesday-Sunday 3:30-6:30pm. Late oysters nightly 7pm-close.  
Conditions: The $1.50 offer is bar-only; its minimum order is not stated. The location page lists daily happy hour only until 6:30pm, so confirm Monday's menu-listed extension. Drink-purchase requirements, tax and gratuity are unspecified.  
[Source](https://themermaidnyc.getbento.com/the-mermaid-inn-times-square-menus/)

### Charlie Bird's nightly Early Bird bar hour

Deals & happy hours — Charlie Bird

Early bar seating offers oysters, fried chicken and pasta alongside reduced-price drinks.

Price: Drink specials $10; oysters $2 each; Tuscan fried chicken $12; fries $10; farro salad $16; gnocchi $22; cacio e pepe $23.  
Schedule: Every night 5-6pm at the bar.  
Conditions: Bar-only. The menu additionally lists Wednesday-Sunday 3-5pm, but the location page does not corroborate that earlier window. Minimum purchases, reservation requirements, tax and gratuity are unspecified.  
[Source](https://www.charliebirdnyc.com/menus/)

### Vin Sur Vingt Midtown East: $40 prix fixe and wine offers

Deals & happy hours — Vin Sur Vingt Bistro

An early two-course bistro meal with soup or pate and mussels or salmon; separate wine offers cover early and late visits.

Price: Prix fixe $40; dessert +$10. Selected wine glasses $10 until 6pm; any wine bottle 25% off from 10pm-close.  
Schedule: Prix fixe 4-7pm; offer-specific weekdays are not stated. Restaurant opens daily at 4pm, closing midnight Sunday-Wednesday and 1am Thursday-Saturday.  
Conditions: HTML lists onion soup or country pate, then mussels and fries or salmon; the $10 dessert add-on offers creme brulee or profiteroles. The linked June 2026-named PDF adds endive salad and a burger and lists seasonal fruit tart instead of profiteroles in its prix-fixe section. Confirm choices; neither version establishes an exact menu revision date. Selected wine glasses are $10 until 6pm; bottles are 25% off from 10pm-close. Holiday eligibility, tax and gratuity are unspecified.  
[Source](https://www.vsvwinebars.com/location/vin-sur-vingt-wine-bar-bistro-e-51st-st/)

### Junoon's Friday-through-Sunday Prestige lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

A comparatively accessible way to try the restaurant, with vegetarian, chicken and seafood choices plus rice and naan.

Price: 2 courses $43; 3 courses $49, including basmati rice and naan.  
Schedule: Friday-Sunday 11:45am-2:30pm.  
Conditions: 20% service charge for parties of 5 or more. The lamb option is marked $7 on the prix-fixe menu; confirm the supplement. Other tax and gratuity treatment is unspecified. The location flags special hours this week without clearly confirming holiday prix-fixe eligibility.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### La Tete d'Or's weekday trolley-cut set lunch

Special menus — La Tete d'Or

A higher-budget set lunch built around a rotating roast rather than ordering steakhouse sides separately.

Price: Trolley Cut of the Week $75.  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 11:30am-2pm.  
Conditions: Includes side Caesar salad, creamy spinach, pommes puree, Bordelaise and Bearnaise sauces, and warm madeleines. The roast rotates. This is an ongoing set menu, not a claimed temporary discount. Tax, gratuity and holiday exclusions are unspecified.  
[Source](https://www.latetedorbydaniel.com/our-menu/lunch)

### Francie's bar happy hour and conditional Monday corkage waiver

Deals & happy hours — Francie

A lower-cost bar introduction to the restaurant, plus a Monday wine offer that requires purchasing a bottle.

Price: Teeny 'Tinis $12; selected wine glasses $13; oysters $10 per listed order; littleneck clams $9 per listed order; olive-oil frites $15.  
Schedule: Monday-Thursday 5:30-7pm; Friday 5-6:30pm. Conditional free corkage on Mondays.  
Conditions: Happy hour is bar-only; shellfish quantities are not specified in that section. Monday free corkage requires purchasing a bottle from the restaurant's list. The menu states regular corkage of $90 per bottle and a two-bottle-per-party limit; confirm how those terms apply to the waiver. A one-bottle-per-two-guests restriction was not established by the reviewed menu. Tax and gratuity are unspecified.  
[Source](https://www.franciebrooklyn.com/menus/)

Deals & happy hours — Canton Harbor

Eater reports lunch combinations with an entree, soup and rice, including beef stew with radish and cashew chicken.

Price: $14-$17, reported by Eater; not independently confirmed on the official website.  
Schedule: Weekday lunch; exact offer hours unknown.  
Conditions: Confirm current price, lunch cutoff and Thanksgiving-week eligibility before relying on the offer. Official site welcomes walk-ins and asks parties of 6 or more to call. Tax, gratuity and other restrictions are unknown.  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-queens-restaurants-heatmap)

The November plat du mois combines duck and foie gras in puff pastry with mushroom farce and truffle-Madeira jus.

Price: $72 for the featured dish.  
Schedule: Dinner throughout November 2026; Monday-Saturday 5-10pm, Sunday 5-9pm.  
Conditions: A separately priced monthly special, not a complete tasting menu or discounted meal. Tax, gratuity and reservation requirements are not specified; nightly availability is unconfirmed.  
From: 2026-11-01  
Through: 2026-11-30  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/experiences)

The dated fall menu features scallop aguachile, chestnut agnolotti, duck with squash and mole, and an apple-pie choco taco.

Price: Tasting menu $130; optional caviar handroll $55.  
Schedule: Current fall menu; service hours unknown.  
Conditions: Cannot accommodate vegan diners, egg, coconut or soybean allergies, or severe allium/herb/spice allergies. Vegetarian accommodation requires eating eggs and dairy. Undisclosed dietary needs are not guaranteed. No outside cakes. Tax and gratuity unspecified.

### Junoon's Prestige and Journey Through India dinners

Special menus — Junoon

Choose a three- or four-course Prestige dinner or the Journey Through India tasting, with optional wine pairings.

Price: Dedicated menu: three-course Prestige $105, pairing $55; four-course Prestige $115, pairing $65; Journey Through India $165, pairing $95. Conflicting location/FAQ copy quotes $85 for three courses and $115 for seven courses; confirm before booking.  
Schedule: Dinner Monday–Thursday and Sunday 5:30–10pm; Friday–Saturday 5:30–10:30pm. Journey Through India excludes Sundays.  
Conditions: Whole-table participation required for Prestige dinner and Journey Through India. Prestige lists supplements including tuna puchka $10, lobster moilee $10 and goat ribs $15. A 20% service charge is added to parties of 5 or more. Tax and other gratuity treatment are unstated. #### [Minor Music at the End of the World — closing performance](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/minor-music)

2026-12-06 · 3:00 PM · theater · From $35 before fees; online fees add $11 per ticket. · BAM Harvey Theater · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 65.0  
Saidiya Hartman's multidisciplinary collaboration, directed by Sarah Benson, features André Holland, Okwui Okpokwasili, and film by Arthur Jafa. Three movements explore Black life and collective possibility after catastrophe.
**Timing:** Final listed performance; 95 minutes without intermission.
**Practical note:** Online single-ticket purchases add $11 in stated fees.
**Why now:** All three performances occur just beyond the weekly window, and the Sunday matinee is the last listed opportunity.
